Be executable: Each action's preconditions satisfied before execution\n2. Reach goal: Final state satisfies all goal predicates\n3. Action name must exactly match domain definition\n2. Parameters must be in correct order\n3. Parameter names must exactly match problem objects\n4. No extra whitespace or commas (except between parameters)\n5. Each action on separate line\n\n## Airport Domain Specific Patterns\n\n### Movement Actions\n```\nmove_seg_SOURCE_0_XSEG_TARGET_0_YFACING_DIR_NEXTDIR_TYPE(airplane_OBJ)\n```\nExample: `move_seg_pp_0_60_seg_ppdoor_0_40_north_north_medium(airplane_CFBEG)`\n\n### Startup Actions\n```\nstartup_seg_SEGMENT_0_XFACING_DIR_TYPE(airplane_OBJ)\n```\nExample: `startup_seg_pp_0_60_north_medium(airplane_CFBEG)`\n\n### Takeoff Actions\n```\ntakeoff_seg_SEGMENT_0_XFACING_DIR(airplane_OBJ)\n```\nExample: `takeoff_seg_rww_0_50_north(airplane_CFBEG)`\n\n### Park Actions\n```\npark_seg_SEGMENT_0_XFACING_DIR(airplane_OBJ)\n```\nExample: `park_seg_pp_0_60_north(airplane_CFBEG)`\n\n## Plan Extraction from Planner Output\n\n### From Fast Downward\n\nFast Downward output format:\n```\nparsing input...\n[parsing output]\nsearch space size: ...\nSolution found!\n[action_name(...)]\n[action_name(...)]\n...\n```\n\nExtraction regex pattern:\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef extract_plan_from_output(output_text):\n    \"\"\"Extract PDDL actions from planner output\"\"\"\n    plan = []\n\n    # Look for lines with action pattern\n    action_pattern = r'(\\w+)\\s*\\(([^)]*)\\)'\n\n    for line in output_text.split('\\n'):\n        # Skip non-action lines\n        if not '(' in line or ')' not in line:\n            continue\n\n        match = re.search(action_pattern, line)\n        if match:\n            action_name = match.group(1)\n            params_str = match.group(2).strip()\n\n            # Extract parameters\n            if params_str:\n                params = [p.strip() for p in params_str.split(',')]\n            else:\n                params = []\n\n            # Format as proper PDDL action\n            action = f\"{action_name}({', '.join(params)})\"\n            plan.append(action)\n\n    return plan\n```\n\n## Validation Checklist\n\nBefore outputting plan:\n- [ ] All actions are defined in domain\n- [ ] All parameters match problem objects\n- [ ] Action sequence is executable (preconditions met)\n- [ ] Final state satisfies all goal conditions\n- [ ] No syntax errors (balanced parentheses, proper formatting)\n- [ ] Each line contains exactly one action\n\n## Output to File\n\n```python\ndef write_plan_to_file(plan, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Write plan to file in correct format\"\"\"\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        for action in plan:\n            # Ensure proper formatting\n            if not action.endswith(')'):\n                action = action.rstrip() + ')'\n            f.write(action + '\\n')\n\n    return output_path\n```\n\n## Common Formatting Errors\n\n1. **Extra spaces**: `move ( plane1 , seg1 )` → should be `move(plane1, seg1)`\n2. **Missing parameters**: `move(plane1)` → should include all parameters\n3. **Wrong parameter order**: Domain defines move(src, dst), not move(dst, src)\n4. **Object name mismatch**: `airplane_A` defined as `airplane_1` in problem\n5. **Incomplete action name**: `move_seg_x` vs full `move_seg_x_y_z_a_b_type`\n\n## Testing Plan Validity\n\n```python\ndef test_plan(domain_file, problem_file, plan_file):\n    \"\"\"Validate plan against domain/problem\"\"\"\n    # Read files\n    with open(domain_file) as f:\n        domain_text = f.read()\n    with open(problem_file) as f:\n        problem_text = f.read()\n    with open(plan_file) as f:\n        plan = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]\n\n    # Basic validation\n    for action_line in plan:\n        # Extract action name\n        action_name = action_line.split('(')[0]\n\n        # Check if action exists in domain\n        if action_name not in domain_text:\n            print(f\"ERROR: Action {action_name} not in domain\")\n            return False\n\n    return True\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1030,"cache_read_input_tokens":36706,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1030,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5aec4727-583b-434c-b56c-258dad1ab76c","uuid":"0889a3c6-e428-4a3d-af1f-2154eb18ecf5"}

Build action string\n    action_string = f\"{action_name}({', '.join(params)})\"\n\n    return action_string, grounded_prec_pos, grounded_prec_neg, grounded_eff_pos, grounded_eff_neg\n\ndef get_all_ground_instances(action_name: str, domain_text: str, objects: List[str]) -> List[Tuple[str, Set[str], Set[str], Set[str], Set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"Generate all ground instances of an action\"\"\"\n    action_text = extract_action_text(domain_text, action_name)\n    if not action_text:\n        return []\n\n    formal_params = get_formal_params(action_text)\n\n    if not formal_params:\n        # Get all ground instances\n        instances = get_all_ground_instances(action_name, domain_text, objects)\n\n        for action_string, prec_pos, prec_neg, eff_pos, eff_neg in instances:\n            # Check if applicable\n            if prec_pos.issubset(state) and prec_neg.isdisjoint(state):\n                applicable.append((action_string, eff_pos, eff_neg))\n\n    return applicable\n\ndef apply_action(state: Set[str], eff_pos: Set[str], eff_neg: Set[str]) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Apply action effects to state\"\"\"\n    new_state = state.copy()\n    new_state.update(eff_pos)\n    new_state -= eff_neg\n    return new_state\n\ndef bfs_search(initial_state: Set[str], goal_state: Set[str], action_names: List[str], domain_text: str, objects: List[str], max_depth: int = 100) -> Optional[List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Breadth-first search for a plan\"\"\"\n\n    # Check if goal is already satisfied\n    if goal_state.issubset(initial_state):\n        return []\n\n    # Initialize queue\n    queue = deque([(initial_state, [])])\n    visited = {frozenset(initial_state)}\n\n    stats = {\n        'states_explored': 0,\n        'actions_checked': 0\n    }\n\n    while queue:\n        current_state, current_plan = queue.popleft()\n\n        stats['states_explored'] += 1\n\n        # Check depth limit\n        if len(current_plan) >= max_depth:\n            continue\n\n        # Print progress\n        if stats['states_explored'] % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"Explored {stats['states_explored']} states, plan length {len(current_plan)}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n        # Get applicable actions\n        applicable = find_applicable_actions(current_state, action_names, domain_text, objects)\n        stats['actions_checked'] += len(applicable)\n\n        for action_string, eff_pos, eff_neg in applicable:\n            new_state = apply_action(current_state, eff_pos, eff_neg)\n            state_key = frozenset(new_state)\n\n            # Check if goal reached\n            if goal_state.issubset(new_state):\n                final_plan = current_plan + [action_string]\n                print(f\"Plan found! Length: {len(final_plan)}, States: {stats['states_explored']}\", file=sys.stderr)\n                return final_plan\n\n            # Add to queue if not visited\n            if state_key not in visited:\n                visited.add(state_key)\n                queue.append((new_state, current_plan + [action_string]))\n\n    print(f\"No plan found. Explored {stats['states_explored']} states, checked {stats['actions_checked']} actions\", file=sys.stderr)\n    return None\n\ndef solve_problem(domain_file: str, problem_file: str) -> Optional[List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Solve a PDDL planning problem\"\"\"\n    domain = read_file(domain_file)\n    problem = read_file(problem_file)\n\n    initial_state = extract_init_facts(problem)\n    goal_state = extract_goal_facts(problem)\n    objects = get_all_objects(domain, problem)\n    action_names = get_action_names(domain)\n\n    print(f\"Parsed problem:\", file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"  Initial state: {len(initial_state)} facts\", file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"  Goal state: {goal_state}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"  Objects: {len(objects)} = {objects}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"  Action templates: {len(action_names)}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    if not goal_state:\n        print(\"ERROR: No goal found!\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return []\n\n    plan = bfs_search(initial_state, goal_state, action_names, domain, objects, max_depth=100)\n    return plan\n\ndef main():\n    with open('/app/problem.json') as f:\n        problems = json.load(f)\n\n    for problem_entry in problems:\n        problem_id = problem_entry['id']\n        domain_file = f\"/app/{problem_entry['domain']}\"\n        problem_file = f\"/app/{problem_entry['problem']}\"\n        output_file = f\"/app/{problem_entry['plan_output']}\"\n\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        print(f\"PROBLEM: {problem_id}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\", From domain constants (multi-line format)\n    match = re.search(r':constants\\s*(.*?)\\)', domain_text, re.DOTALL)\n    if match:\n        text = match.group(1)\n        lines = text.split('\\n')\n        for line in lines:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            # Check if this line contains a type definition (has ' - ')\n            if ' - ' in line:\n                # Split by the type marker\n                parts = line.split(' - ')\n                objs_part = parts[0].strip()\n                # All words in this part are objects\n                objs = objs_part.split()\n                objects.extend(objs)\n            else:\n                # This line has objects without type (type is on next line)\n                objs = line.split()\n                objects.extend(objs)\n\n    # From problem objects\n    match = re.search(r':objects\\s*(.*?)\\)', problem_text, re.DOTALL)\n    if match:\n        text = match.group(1)\n        lines = text.split('\\n')\n        for line in lines:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            if ' - ' in line:\n                parts = line.split(' - ')\n                objs_part = parts[0].strip()\n                objs = objs_part.split()\n                objects.extend(objs)\n            else:\n                objs = line.split()\n                objects.extend(objs)\n\n    return sorted(list(set(objects)))\n\ndef get_action_names(domain_text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract all action names\"\"\"\n    return re.findall(r':action\\s+(\\w+)', domain_text)\n\ndef extract_action_text(domain_text: str, action_name: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract the full text of an action definition\"\"\"\n    # Find the action :action keyword\n    pattern = rf':action\\s+{re.escape(action_name)}\\s'\n    match = re.search(pattern, domain_text)\n    if not match:\n        return None\n\n    start = match.start()\n\n    # Find the end - next :action or end of parentheses\n    next_action = domain_text.find(':action', match.end())\n    if next_action == -1:\n        # Find the last closing paren before the next definition keyword\n        end = len(domain_text)\n    else:\n        end = next_action\n\n    return domain_text[start:end]\n\ndef get_formal_params(action_text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract formal parameter names from action\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r':parameters\\s*\\((.*?)\\)', action_text, re.DOTALL)\n    if not match:\n        return []\n\n    params_str = match.group(1)\n    # Find all ?name patterns\n    params = re.findall(r'\\?(\\w+)', params_str)\n    return params\n\ndef get_preconditions(action_text: str) -> Tuple[Set[str], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Extract preconditions (positive and negative)\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r':precondition\\s*\\((.*?)\\)\\s*:effect', action_text, re.DOTALL)\n    if not match:\n        return set(), set()\n\n    precond_text = match.group(1)\n    positive = set()\n    negative = set()\n\n    # Extract all (fact) patterns\n    for fact in re.findall(r'\\(([^)]+)\\)', precond_text):\n        fact = fact.strip()\n        # Skip empty and 'and'\n        if not fact or fact == 'and':\n            continue\n        # Check if it's negated\n        if fact.startswith('not '):\n            # Extract the actual fact from (not (...))\n            negative.add(fact[4:].strip())\n        else:\n            positive.add(fact)\n\n    return positive, negative\n\ndef get_effects(action_text: str) -> Tuple[Set[str], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Extract effects (add and delete)\"\"\"\n    # Find the :effect keyword\n    effect_idx = action_text.find(':effect')\n    if effect_idx == -1:\n        return set(), set()\n\n    # Start after :effect\n    start_idx = action_text.find('(', effect_idx)\n    if start_idx == -1:\n        return set(), set()\n\n    # Find matching closing paren\n    depth = 0\n    end_idx = start_idx\n    for i in range(start_idx, len(action_text)):\n        if action_text[i] == '(':\n            depth += 1\n        elif action_text[i] == ')':\n            depth -= 1\n            if depth == 0:\n                end_idx = i\n                break\n\n    effect_text = action_text[start_idx+1:end_idx]\n\n    positive = set()\n    negative = set()\n\n    # Extract all (fact) patterns\n    for fact in re.findall(r'\\(([^)]+)\\)', effect_text):\n        fact = fact.strip()\n        # Skip empty and 'and'\n        if not fact or fact == 'and':\n            continue\n        # Check if it's negated\n        if fact.startswith('not '):\n            negative.add(fact[4:].strip())\n        else:\n            positive.add(fact)\n\n    return positive, negative\n\ndef substitute_variables(text: str, substitution: Dict[str, str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Replace variables with objects in a text\"\"\"\n    result = text\n    for var, obj in substitution.items():\n        result = result.replace(var, obj)\n    return result\n\ndef ground_action(action_name: str, params: Tuple[str, ...], domain_text: str) -> Optional[Tuple[str, Set[str], Set[str], Set[str], Set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Ground an action with concrete parameters.\n    Returns: (action_string, precond_positive, precond_negative, effect_positive, effect_negative)\n    \"\"\"\n    action_text = extract_action_text(domain_text, action_name)\n    if not action_text:\n        return None\n\n    formal_params = get_formal_params(action_text)\n\n    # Check parameter count\n    if len(formal_params) != len(params):\n        return None\n\n    # Build substitution dictionary\n    substitution = {f\"?{formal}\": param for formal, param in zip(formal_params, params)}\n\n    # Get preconditions and effects\n    prec_pos, prec_neg = get_preconditions(action_text)\n    eff_pos, eff_neg = get_effects(action_text)\n\n    # Substitute variables\n    grounded_prec_pos = {substitute_variables(fact, substitution) for fact in prec_pos}\n    grounded_prec_neg = {substitute_variables(fact, substitution) for fact in prec_neg}\n    grounded_eff_pos = {substitute_variables(fact, substitution) for fact in eff_pos}\n    grounded_eff_neg = {substitute_variables(fact, substitution) for fact in eff_neg}\n\n    # Build action string\n    action_string = f\"{action_name}({', '.join(params)})\"\n\n    return action_string, grounded_prec_pos, grounded_prec_neg, grounded_eff_pos, grounded_eff_neg\n\ndef get_all_ground_instances(action_name: str, domain_text: str, objects: List[str]) -> List[Tuple[str, Set[str], Set[str], Set[str], Set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"Generate all ground instances of an action\"\"\"\n    action_text = extract_action_text(domain_text, action_name)\n    if not action_text:\n        return []\n\n    formal_params = get_formal_params(action_text)\n\n    if not formal_params:\n        # Get all ground instances\n        instances = get_all_ground_instances(action_name, domain_text, objects)\n\n        for action_string, prec_pos, prec_neg, eff_pos, eff_neg in instances:\n            # Check if applicable\n            if prec_pos.issubset(state) and prec_neg.isdisjoint(state):\n                applicable.append((action_string, eff_pos, eff_neg))\n\n    return applicable\n\ndef apply_action(state: Set[str], eff_pos: Set[str], eff_neg: Set[str]) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Apply action effects to state\"\"\"\n    new_state = state.copy()\n    new_state.update(eff_pos)\n    new_state -= eff_neg\n    return new_state\n\ndef bfs_search(initial_state: Set[str], goal_state: Set[str], action_names: List[str], domain_text: str, objects: List[str], max_depth: int = 100) -> Optional[List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Breadth-first search for a plan\"\"\"\n\n    # Check if goal is already satisfied\n    if goal_state.issubset(initial_state):\n        return []\n\n    # Initialize queue\n    queue = deque([(initial_state, [])])\n    visited = {frozenset(initial_state)}\n\n    stats = {\n        'states_explored': 0,\n        'actions_checked': 0\n    }\n\n    while queue:\n        current_state, current_plan = queue.popleft()\n\n        stats['states_explored'] += 1\n\n        # Check depth limit\n        if len(current_plan) >= max_depth:\n            continue\n\n        # Print progress\n        if stats['states_explored'] % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"Explored {stats['states_explored']} states, plan length {len(current_plan)}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n        # Get applicable actions\n        applicable = find_applicable_actions(current_state, action_names, domain_text, objects)\n        stats['actions_checked'] += len(applicable)\n\n        for action_string, eff_pos, eff_neg in applicable:\n            new_state = apply_action(current_state, eff_pos, eff_neg)\n            state_key = frozenset(new_state)\n\n            # Length: {len(final_plan)}, States: {stats['states_explored']}\", file=sys.stderr)\n                return final_plan\n\n            # Add to queue if not visited\n            if state_key not in visited:\n                visited.add(state_key)\n                queue.append((new_state, current_plan + [action_string]))\n\n    print(f\"No plan found. Explored {stats['states_explored']} states, checked {stats['actions_checked']} actions\", file=sys.stderr)\n    return None\n\ndef solve_problem(domain_file: str, problem_file: str) -> Python PDDL Planning Tools (`environment/skills/python-pddl-planning.md`)\n- Python libraries for PDDL (pddl, pyperplan)\n- Plan parsing and validation\n- Alternative subprocess-based execution\n- Debugging techniques\n\n### 4. PDDL Plan Extraction (`environment/skills/pddl-plan-extraction.md`)\n- Plan format specifications\n- Airport domain action patterns\n- Plan extraction from planner output\n- Validation and formatting\n\n## Solution Implementation\n\n### Main Solver: `/app/solve_pddl_final.py`\n\nThe solver implements a breadth-first search planner with the following components:\n\n#### Key Functions:\n1. **Parsing Functions**:\n   - `extract_init_facts()`: Parse initial state from problem file\n   - `extract_goal_facts()`: Parse goal from problem file\n   - `get_all_objects()`: Extract objects from domain constants and problem objects (handles multi-line PDDL format)\n   - `get_action_names()`: Extract action template names\n\n2. **Action Processing**:\n   - `extract_action_text()`: Get full action definition\n   - `get_formal_params()`: Extract parameter names from action\n   - `get_preconditions()`: Parse positive and negative preconditions\n   - `get_effects()`: Parse add and delete effects (handles nested parentheses correctly)\n   - `ground_action()`: Create ground instances with specific parameters\n   - `get_all_ground_instances()`: Generate all ground instances of an action\n\n3. **Planning Functions**:\n   - `find_applicable_actions()`: Determine which actions can execute in current state\n   - `apply_action()`: Apply action effects to state\n   - `bfs_search()`: Breadth-first search for plan\n\n#### Key Technical Solutions:\n- **Multi-line parsing**: Handles PDDL format where objects are declared on multiple lines before type specification\n- **Nested parenthesis handling**: Correctly extracts effects even with nested `(not (...))` patterns\n- **State representation**: Uses sets of facts for efficient subset checking\n- **State space search**: BFS explores reachable states until goal is found\n\n## Results\n\n### Task 01: `task01.txt`\n**Goal**: Park airplane_CFBEG at seg_pp_0_60\n\n**Plan** (8 actions):\n1.
